Projected gradient details:
Variables h length n. Target sum S = n/2. Constraint box [0,1] and equality sum S.
Projection P(h): We need project onto {0≤h≤1, sum=hS}. Standard:
- lower=0, upper=1.
- If h sum within? Use algorithm: For equality sum with box, find t such that sum(clip(h_i + t,0,1))=S. If h has box but wrong sum, monotone. We can use binary search on t in [min(h)-max?]. Simpler: Start with h clipped; if sum > S, reduce largest values; if sum < S increase smallest. Since one equality, can sort. But we need many projections; sort O(n log n) per step acceptable for n<=1280 and steps thousands? Maybe.
Alternative maintain sum exact in optimization by subtracting mean after clip? Need box. We can implement efficient projection:
def project_box_sum(x, target):
    x = np.asarray(x, float)
    lo = 0; hi=1
    # if already close and within bounds? 
    y = np.clip(x, lo, hi)
    diff = target - y.sum()
    if abs(diff) <= 1e-12: return y
    if diff > 0: need increase variables below 1
       capacities = 1 - y
       # distribute diff proportionally to capacities? Clipping may need iterative.
    else decrease.
Simplest exact: Find threshold tau for y_i = clip(x_i + tau,0,1). Use binary search tau. n small. Number of projections maybe 1000, binary search 30*O(n) =30k per n, okay.
Pseudo:
def project_box_sum(x, target):
    x = x.copy()
    # If sum out of range? target between 0 and n.
    def sum_for_tau(t):
       y = np.clip(x+t,0,1); return y.sum()
    lo = -x.max() - 1e-9? To get sum <= target; hi = 1 - x.min() +1e-9.
    # Actually if x has values outside, clip x+tau. Use lo=min(x)-? We'll binary search on tau.
    # We can bound tau: low = min(0, target - x.sum()) - 1, high = ... but okay.
    lo = - (x.max() - target?) not important; do 60 iterations.
    for _ in range(60): tau=.5*(lo+hi); s=sum_for_tau(tau); if s < target: lo=tau else hi=tau.
    y=np.clip(x+tau,0,1)
    # due rounding, adjust on a free variable with capacity in direction to make sum exactly target? Could set one variable: free = (y>0)&(y<1); if target-sum in error, adjust free. But if none (all 0/1), sum might not match if target not integer/target? target integer; if all 0/1 and sum not target then no feasible projection? The projection by threshold should yield sum=target within eps. Floating error maybe 1e-12. We can adjust one free variable by diff if within capacity.
    return y

But binary search on x+t for all x each time O(60n). Could be fine. For n=1280, 76k operations per projection; 1000 = 76M okay.

Gradient for smooth objective:
Compute vals and weights. Need stable softmax over vals. vals length M=2n-1: plus s=0..n-1 and neg s=1..n-1.
But need ensure vals correspond exactly to np.correlate? We compute c_pos, c_neg. They are counts (unscaled). Since F=max(c)*dx, minimizing c. Use logsumexp on c counts (units) might have large values ~n/4; beta dimension? We can scale by beta. Smooth objective S = (1/beta) logsumexp(beta*c). But beta large relative to counts? c ~ n/4. We want approximate max over c. If beta=1000 and c differences ~n? For n=512, differences maybe 100, beta*c huge overflow. Need stable with max c subtract; beta*(c-cmax). If beta 10, width ~0.1 counts; C5 dx scale: 0.1*2/512=0.0004. Good. Use beta relative to counts. Or define vals = c * dx (overlap integrals ~0.5); beta ~1000; differences 0.01 -> beta diff=10. Use scaled values to avoid overflow. Let's work with r = c * dx (units overlap). Max ~0.5, differences maybe 0.1. Smooth object logsumexp(beta*(r - rmax)). beta 50-500. Gradient wrt h of r_s is dx * grad_c. Sum weighted by softmax over r. Good.
Use r_pos = (prefix[n-s] - a_s) * dx; r_neg = (U - prefix[s] - a_s) * dx. vals = concatenate(r_pos, r_neg[1:]). c0 included in r_pos[0]. (r_neg[0] duplicate; omit). M=2n-1.
softmax:
shift = vals - vals.max()
z = np.exp(beta*shift)
w = z / z.sum()
G = dx * ∑ w_pos * gc_pos + ∑ w_neg * gc_neg.
Need gradient of r_s. We can compute via direct loops over all s using weights; for n=256, 511 weights, loop with vector operations length n -> 130k operations per gradient. Fine. Could implement direct vectorized per s using slicing:
G = zeros
for s in range(n):
   wp = w_pos[s]
   if wp:
      # mass: if s==0 ones else zeros; G[:n-s] += wp
      if s==0: G += wp
      else: G[:n-s] += wp
      # cross forward: j=0..n-1-s: -h[j+s]
      G[:n-s] -= wp * h[s:]
      # cross backward: j=s..n-1: -h[j-s]
      G[s:] -= wp * h[:n-s]
for s in range(1,n):
   wn = w_neg[s]
   if wn:
      G[s:] += wn  # mass suffix
      G[:n-s] -= wn * h[s:]
      G[s:] -= wn * h[:n-s]
This is O(n^2) but vector C operations per s with Python loop n. For n=512, 512 loops, 260k element ops, maybe 0.001? Python loop overhead 512* maybe 0.0001? Total ~0.005s per gradient. 1000 grads ->5s. n=1280 -> 1.6M ops per grad, 1.5ms? 1000 ->15s plus overhead maybe okay. But scipy FFT eval also.

Fast eval direct loop:
def overlap_values_counts(h):
   n=len(h); pref=np.empty(n+1); pref[0]=0; np.cumsum(h, out=pref[1:])
   vals = np.empty(2*n-1)
   vals[0] = h[:n]? c0 = sum(h) - np.dot(h,h)
   # positive s: index maybe s
   for s in range(1,n):
       a = np.dot(h[:n-s], h[s:])
       vals_pos[s] = pref[n-s] - a
       vals_neg[s] = pref[n] - pref[s] - a
   max.
This computes both. It can be used for objective and softmax weights. It also gives vals array for gradient weights. Good.

But gradient direct needs weights by s; we can use vals_pos/vals_neg. We can combine val_pos length n and val_neg length n. For s=0 c0. Good.

Gradient function using weights on pos/neg lags (r weights). Implementation direct all weights maybe:
def gradient_smooth(h, wp, wn, dx):
   n=len(h); g=np.zeros(n)
   # pos s=0
   if wp[0]:
      t=wp[0]
      g[:] += t  # mass
      g[:] -= 2*t*h
   # could vector loop s=1..n-1 for pos and neg
   for s in range(1,n):
      t=wp[s]
      if t:
         g[:n-s] += t
         g[:n-s] -= t*h[s:]
         g[s:] -= t*h[:n-s]
      t=wn[s]
      if t:
         g[s:] += t
         g[:n-s] -= t*h[s:]
         g[s:] -= t*h[:n-s]
   g *= dx
   return g

But note for s=0 in neg omitted; we set wn[0]=0.
If many tiny weights, skip threshold. But if using logsumexp, weights sum 1; can skip <1e-8.

Let's design projected gradient with adaptive step:
A method for projection onto convex set C. For step size L, x_{new}=proj(x - grad/L). We need estimate Lipschitz constant of G_beta over C. Could compute max abs grad and use lr=0.1 / max_abs? Since grad scale ~dx, max_abs ~0.01, lr=10. Use backtracking.
Pseudo:
def optimize_candidate(h0, beta_schedule, max_iter, time_limit):
   h=project(h0,target)
   bestG=evalG(h,beta)
   bestF=exact/fastF(h)
   lr=10.0
   no_improve=0
   for it in range(max_iter):
      if time up: break
      G, vals = eval_vals(h,beta)
      if G < bestG - 1e-9: bestG=G
      # compute weights for current beta
      w = softmax(vals,beta)
      g = grad_fft(h,w[:,pos],w[:,neg],dx)
      normg = max abs g; if normg < 1e-12 break
      # gradient can be zero; perturb
      improved=False
      for factor in [1.0, 0.5, 0.25, 0.125, 0.0625]:
          step = lr*factor*? Maybe normalize g by normg? If using lr as reciprocal, step = lr*g. But if normg around 0.01 and lr=10 step .1. Good.
          h_new = project(h - lr*factor*g, target)
          G_new, _ = evalG(h_new,beta)
          # accept if G_new <= G - 1e-10 (or F improvement)
          if G_new < G - 1e-12:
              h=h_new; G=G_new; improved=True; break
          # also if F_new < bestF? Could update best but not move? Maybe if G_old < G_new but F_new lower? Hard.
      if not improved:
          # try small random perturbation if stuck
          if lr > 1e-3: lr *= 0.5
          else: break
      else:
          # increase lr slightly
          lr = min(lr*1.05, 1000)
      # update bestF using fast exact or FFT
      F=fast_F(h); if F<bestF: best=h.copy(); no=0 else no++; if no>50 maybe perturb.
   return best.

Functions:

def _fft_len(n): return 1 << (2*n - 1).bit_length()  # maybe (2*n -1).bit_length

For evaluation:
```
def fast_overlap_counts(h):
    n = h.size
    N = 1 << (2*n - 1).bit_length()
    H = np.fft.rfft(h, N)
    ac = np.fft.irfft(H * np.conj(H), N)
    a = ac[:n]
    # clean
    a = np.where(a < 0 and > -1e-12, 0, a)
    pref = np.empty(n+1); pref[0]=0; np.cumsum(h, out=pref[1:])
    U = pref[n]
    s = np.arange(n)
    pos = pref[n - s] - a
    neg = U - pref[s] - a
    pos = np.maximum(pos,0); neg=np.maximum(neg,0)
    return pos, neg, float(max(pos.max(),neg.max()))
```
For exact F:
```
def exact_c5(h):
   n=len(h); dx=2.0/n
   return float(np.max(np.correlate(h, 1.0 - h, mode='full')) * dx)
```
Could exact correlate be slow for large n (2048 maybe 8M C ops, one time fine).
Fast F = fast_overlap_counts(h)[2]*dx. Note due FFT error might slightly under/over; use exact for best updates? For speed line search fast. For final best, exact.

Need be careful with logsumexp overflow. vals r in [0,0.5]. beta maybe up to 1000. Use max_shift.
```
def softmax_r(pos,neg,beta):
    n=len(pos)
    vals = np.empty(2*n-1)
    vals[:n]=pos
    vals[n:]=neg[1:]
    m=vals.max()
    z=np.exp(beta*(vals-m))
    zsum=z.sum()
    if zsum<1e-300: w = zeros; w[argmax]=1
    else: w = z/zsum
    return vals, w, m
```
G = m*dx? Wait pos/neg are counts? We can make them r values by multiplying dx. Let's do counts for eval; then r = counts*dx. For softmax use r. Simpler: fast returns counts; when compute G, max_r = max_count*dx. z = exp(beta*(r - max_r)). G = dx*(max_count + (1/beta)*log(sum exp(beta*(counts - max_count))))? Since r difference = dx*(counts - maxcount). Beta_r on r. Could choose beta_r. Let vals_r = pos*dx. Use directly.
```
def eval_smooth(h,beta):
   posc,negc,c_maxc = fast_overlap_counts(h)
   dx=2/n
   vals = np.empty(2*n-1); vals[:n]=posc*dx; vals[n:]=negc[1:]*dx
   m=vals.max()
   z=np.exp(beta*(vals-m)); zsum=z.sum()
   G=m + (1/beta)*np.log(zsum)
   w=z/zsum
   return G, w, posc, negc
```
If beta large, z underflow for nonmax; okay. If beta small maybe zsum large but bounded exp(0) sum M.

def optimize_smooth(h, time_limit, beta_schedule, init_lr=None):
    h = project_box_sum(h)
    best = h.copy(); bestF = fastF(h)
    lr = 20.0  # maybe
    for beta in schedule:
       G, w = eval_smooth(h,beta)
       F=fastF
       boom=0; noimp=0
       for it in range(maxit):
          if time up break
          w_alpha from current? If beta fixed.
          g = grad_fft(h,w, dx)
          ng = np.max(np.abs(g))
          if ng < 1e-12: break
          # choose lrs: [lr, lr*0.5, lr*0.25, lr*0.125]
          accepted=False
          for fac in [1,0.5,0.25,0.125,0.0625]:
              step = lr*fac
              # Armijo target: G_new <= G - c*step*grad^T(g-h)? For projection use G_new <= G - 1e-8
              h_new = project(h - step*g)
              G_new = evalG(h_new)
              F_new = fastF(h_new)
              if G_new <= G - 1e-11 or F_new < F - 1e-10:
                  accepted=True; h=h_new; break
              # maybe store best_trial if F_new<F but G not? If F_new<F accept? Already condition.
          if accepted:
             G = G_new; F=F_new; noimp=0
             lr = min(lr*1.03, 200.0)
          else:
             lr *= 0.5
             noimp +=1
             if lr < 1e-4:
                # perturb
                break
          if F < bestF:
             bestF=F; best=h.copy(); noimp=0
          if noimp>30: break
       # after beta, project fallback
    return best,bestF
```

Upsampling function:
Mapping step function: parent samples represent cells over [0,2]. To upsample to n (not necessarily multiple), for each i in 0..n-1, x_center=(i+0.5)*2/n. Parent cell index j = floor(x_center / (2/n0)) = floor((i+0.5)*n0/n). Clip j to n0-1. h[i]=parent[j]. If n multiple, exact sum (if parent sum target/n0? yes). If not, may not exact; project. But we'll use multiples of n0 to avoid. Also if n0 not dividing target? n0 even, parent sum n0/2. Up factor integer. Good.

```
def project_box_sum(x, target):
    n=x.size
    # find tau bounds
    lo = target - x.sum() - n  # lower? Actually if tau very negative, sum 0; tau very positive, sum n.
    lo = min(0.0, target - x.mean()) - 1.0? Since tau additive. We can just set lo = -x.max() - 1.0? To ensure c=0, need x+lo <=0 for all => lo <= -max(x). hi >= 1-min(x) to ensure sum n.
    lo = -np.max(x) - 1e-9
    hi = 1.0 - np.min(x) + 1e-9
    # If target near 0/n, bounds ok.
    for _ in range(50):
       mid=(lo+hi)/2
       s=np.clip(x+mid,0,1).sum()
       if s < target: lo=mid
       else: hi=mid
    y=np.clip(x+(lo+hi)/2,0,1)
    # adjust for floating exact: diff = target - y.sum()
    diff = target - y.sum()
    if abs(diff) > 1e-12:
       # adjust free variables
       if diff >0:
          free = np.where((y > 0) & (y < 1))[0]
          if free.size: y[free[0]] += diff # may exceed? if diff tiny.
       else:
          free = ...
          y[free[0]] += diff
       y=np.clip(y,0,1)
    return y
```
But binary search with float x may be inaccurate if target 40.0 and y values; 50 enough (range ~100, 2^-50 tiny). Could be 40 enough.

Upsampling:
```
def upsample_step(h, n):
    m=len(h)
    if n==m: return h.copy()
    # cell centers approach
    idx = np.floor((np.arange(n)+0.5)*m/n).astype(int)
    idx = np.clip(idx,0,m-1)
    return h[idx].astype(float)
```
If n multiple, this maps each new cell to parent cell. Example m=2,n=4: centers 0.125,0.375,0.625,0.875 => idx [0,0? floor .25? Wait (i+0.5)*m/n: i0 .25 floor0; i1 .75 floor0; i2 1.25 floor1; i3 1.75 floor1. Good. For m=80,n=160: each parent 2 cells. Sum exact.

def random_smooth(n, seed):
    rng=np.random.default_rng(seed)
    base = rng.standard_normal(max(16,n//16))
    h = np.interp(np.linspace(0.5, n-0.5, n), np.linspace(0.5, len(base)-0.5, len(base)), base)
    h = (h - h.min())/(h.max()-h.min()+1e-12)

Exact objective:
```
def exact_c5(h):
    h = np.asarray(h, dtype=float)
    n = h.size
    dx = 2.0 / n
    vals = np.correlate(h, 1.0 - h, mode='full')
    return float(np.max(vals) * dx)
```
`np.correlate` for large n maybe returns float64. Good.

Fast counts:
```
def overlap_counts_fast(h):
    h = np.asarray(h, dtype=float)
    n = h.size
    N = 1 << (2 * n - 1).bit_length()
    H = np.fft.rfft(h, N)
    ac = np.fft.irfft(H * np.conj(H), N)
    a = ac[:n]
    # remove tiny negative
    if a.size:
        amin = a.min()
        if amin < 0 and amin > -1e-9:
            a = np.maximum(a, 0.0)
        elif amin < -1e-4:
            a = np.maximum(a, 0.0) # maybe still
    pref = np.empty(n+1); pref[0]=0.0; np.cumsum(h, out=pref[1:])
    U = pref[n]
    # Using vector s
    s = np.arange(n)
    pos = pref[n - s] - a
    neg = U - pref[s] - a
    # positive clip
    pos = np.maximum(pos, 0.0)
    neg = np.maximum(neg, 0.0)
    return pos, neg
```
But `pref[n - s]` for s=0..n-1 yields pref n, n-1,...1. Need length n. Good.
`neg = U - pref[s]` for s=0..n-1: pref[0]=0, pref[1]... pref[n-1]. Good.

Fast F:
```
def fast_c5(h):
   pos,neg=overlap_counts_fast(h)
   n=h.size
   return float(max(pos.max(), neg.max()) * (2.0/n))
```
For n=0? no.

Smooth eval:
```
def smooth_stats(h, beta):
    n=h.size; dx=2.0/n
    pos, neg = overlap_counts_fast(h)
    vals = np.empty(2*n - 1, dtype=float)
    vals[:n] = pos * dx
    vals[n:] = neg[1:] * dx
    m = float(np.max(vals))
    ya = beta * (vals - m)
    # clip for overflow? ya <=0. But if beta huge and vals-m large positive? no because subtract max.
    z = np.exp(ya)
    zsum = float(z.sum())
    if zsum <= 0 or not np.isfinite(zsum):
        w = np.zeros(vals.size); idx=int(np.argmax(vals)); w[idx]=1.0
        G=m
    else:
        w = z / zsum
        G = m + (1.0/beta) * float(np.log(zsum))
    return G, w, m
```
Need if beta=0? no.

def smooth_gradient(h, w_vals, dx):
    n = h.size
    # if n==1: gradient of c0 only? not relevant.
    wp = w_vals[:n].astype(float)  # copy
    wn = np.zeros(n, dtype=float)
    if n > 1:
        wn[1:] = w_vals[n:]
    # mass
    mass = np.zeros(n, dtype=float)
    wp0 = float(wp[0]) if n>0 else 0.0
    if n > 1:
        # left_cum pos
        left = np.empty(n, dtype=float)
        left[0] = wp[1:].sum()
        if n > 2:
            # left[j] = sum wp[1:n-j]
            # compute cumulative from left? Simpler:
            cum = np.cumsum(wp[1:][::-1])  # maybe not

        # We want left[j] for j=0..n-1: sum_{s=1}^{n-1-j} wp[s]
        # reversed = wp[1:][::-1] = [wp[n-1],...,wp[1]]

        # Thus left[j] = total_w - (cum_rev[j-1] if j>0 else 0).
        total_w = wp[1:].sum()
        if total_w > 0:

            cumrev = np.cumsum(rev)

            mass_pos = wp0 + left

    else:
        mass = np.full(n, wp0)
    # neg mass
    if n > 1:
        # neg_mass[j] = sum_{s=1}^{j} wn[s]
        cumwn = np.cumsum(wn[1:])  # length n-1
        negmass = np.zeros(n)
        negmass[1:] = cumwn
        mass += negmass
    g = mass
    # subtract cross via FFT
    wc = wp + wn
    if np.any(wc):
       L = 1 << (2 * n - 1).bit_length()
       K = np.zeros(L)

           K[:0:-1] = wc[1:][::-1]

def smooth_value_only(h,beta):
   pos,neg=overlap_counts_fast(h)
   n=h.size; dx=2.0/n
   vals... (or avoid constructing? We can compute m/sum from pos,neg maybe)
   mpos=pos.max(); mneg=neg[1:].max() if n>1 else -1; m=max(mpos,mneg)*dx
   z = exp(beta*(pos*dx - m)) + exp(beta*(neg[1:]*dx - m))
   G = m + log(z.sum())/beta
   return G, m
```

def optimize_candidate(h0, budget_s, beta_schedule=(40,100,250,500), alpha_init=0.05):
    h = np.asarray(h0,float)
    n=h.size
    h = project_box_sum(h, n/2.0)
    best_h = h.copy()
    best_F = fast_c5(h)
    start=time.time(); end=start+budget_s-2
    alpha = alpha_init
    for beta in beta_schedule:
        G, w, m = smooth_stats(h,beta)
        F = m
        noimp=0
        it=0
        while time.time() < end and noimp<40 and it<20000:
            it+=1
            # update weights maybe current G? Since h changed, need recompute at top of loop? We compute initial; after accepted, we have G_new but weights outdated. We should recompute weights at start each iteration. So structure while:
            G, w, m = smooth_stats(h,beta)
            F = m
            if F < best_F: best_F=F; best_h=h.copy(); noimp=0
            if time up break
            g = smooth_gradient(h,w,2.0/n)
            gmax = np.max(np.abs(g))
            if gmax <= 1e-12: break
            d = g/gmax
            accepted=False
            # Try alpha values
            for fac in (1.0,0.5,0.25,0.125,0.0625,0.03125):
                h2 = project_box_sum(h - alpha*fac*d, n/2.0)
                G2, F2 = smooth_value_only(h2,beta)
                if (G2 < G - 1e-12) or (F2 < F - 1e-10):
                    G = G2; F = F2; h = h2; accepted=True; break
            if accepted:
                alpha = min(alpha*1.05, 0.25)
                noimp=0
            else:
                alpha *= 0.7
                noimp += 1
                if alpha < 1e-4:
                    break
        # if no improvement many, maybe perturb
        if noimp>0 and time left:
           # add small symmetric? random perturbation preserving sum
           h = add_perturbation(h, n, 0.01, rng?) but optimize_candidate no rng? Use deterministic? We can pass rng.
```

Now final rounding to dyadic.
```
def make_dyadic_feasible(h, D=1<<20):
    n=h.size
    a = np.floor(h * D + 1e-12).astype(np.int64)
    # due h maybe >1 after clip? clip first
    h = np.clip(h,0,1)
    a = np.clip(a,0,D)
    frac = h*D - a
    target = (n//2)*D  # n even
    need = int(round(target - a.sum()))
    # need could be slightly not equal sum frac due float. Clamp/ adjust.
    if need > n: need=n
    if need < -n: need=-n
    if need > 0:
       # choose indices with largest frac where a<D
       order = np.argsort(-frac)
       added=0
       for idx in order:
          if added>=need: break
          if a[idx] < D:
             a[idx]+=1; added+=1
       if added < need:
          # still need, add to any with capacity (frac maybe negative?) though sum target issue
          rem = np.where(a < D)[0]
          a[rem[:need-added]] +=1
    elif need < 0:
       need = -need
       order = np.argsort(frac)  # smallest frac
       sub=0
       for idx in order:
          if sub>=need: break
          if a[idx] > 0:
             a[idx]-=1; sub+=1
       if sub < need:
          rem = np.where(a > 0)[0]
          a[rem[:need-sub]] -=1
    return a.astype(float)/float(D)
```

def candidate_ns(n0, max_n=2048):
    ns = set()
    for mult in [1,2,4,8,16]:
       n = n0*mult
       if 2 <= n <= max_n: ns.add(n)
    for p in [128,256,512,1024,2048]:
       if 2 <= p <= max_n and p % 2 ==0: ns.add(p)
    return sorted(ns)
```

Mass pos vector code: Let's implement cleanly:
```
def gradient_smooth(h, w_vals, dx):
    n=h.size
    wp = np.asarray(w_vals[:n], dtype=float)
    wn = zeros(n)
    if n>1: wn[1:] = w_vals[n:]
    mass = np.zeros(n)
    wp0 = wp[0]
    mass += wp0
    if n > 1:
        total = float(wp[1:].sum())
        if total > 0:
            left = np.empty(n)
            left[0] = total
            if n > 2:
                revcum = np.cumsum(wp[1:][::-1])
                left[1:] = total - revcum[:n-1]
            mass += left
        # neg mass
        total_neg = float(wn[1:].sum())
        if total_neg > 0:
            cumwn = np.cumsum(wn[1:])
            mass[1:] += cumwn
    # cross
    wc = wp + wn
    if np.any(wc):
       L=...
       K=zeros
       K[0]=2*wc[0]
       if n>1:
          K[1:n]=wc[1:]
          idx=np.arange(1,n)
          K[L-idx]=wc[idx]
       cross=irfft(rfft(K)*rfft(h), L)[:n]
       mass -= cross
    mass *= dx
    return mass
```
Check `left[1:] = total - revcum[:n-1]`: For n=5, left[1:] length4, revcum length4, okay. For n=2, if total>0, left[0]=total, left[1:] empty; pos mass j0 total, j1 0. We initialized mass += wp0, so j0 wp0+total. Good. But if total=0 and n>1, left not added; mass all wp0, correct. For n=5, left[1]=total - wp[4] (sum s=1..3); left[2]=total - (wp4+wp3)=sum1..2; left[3]=total-(wp4+wp3+wp2)=wp1; left[4]=total-total=0. Good.
What about wc[0] = wp0+wn0, wn0=0. K0=2wp0. Good.

Potential problem: w_vals length 2n-1. For n=1, w_vals length1; wn size1; wn[1:] empty. mass=wp0. wc0=wp0. L=1<<(1).bit_length=2. K[0]=2wp0. cross h*w? For n=1, cross = 2wp0 h0. G=dx(wp0-2wp0 h0)=dx wp0(1-2h0), correct (only lag0). But target sum=0.5? n=1 not feasible with h in [0,1] and sum=0.5? h=0.5; lag0=0.25, C=0.5? But n=1 irrelevant.

def initial_random(n, seed):

```
def optimize_candidate(h0, budget_s, rng=None, beta_schedule=None, alpha_init=0.04):
    h = project_box_sum(h0,...)
    best_h=h.copy(); best_F=fast_c5(h)
    start=time.time(); end=start+budget_s
    if beta_schedule is None: beta_schedule=[30,80,200,500]
    factors=(1.0,0.5,0.25,0.125,0.0625,0.03125,0.015625)
    for beta in beta_schedule:
       if time.time() > end-5: break
       alpha=alpha_init
       stall=0
       while time.time() < end-5:
           G,w,F = smooth_stats(h,beta)
           if F < best_F - 1e-12:
               best_F=F; best_h=h.copy(); stall=0
           else:
               stall += 1
           if stall > 150: break
           g=smooth_gradient(...)
           sc = np.max(np.abs(g))
           if sc < 1e-12: break
           d=g/sc
           accepted=False
           for fac in factors:
               step = alpha*fac
               h2 = project_box_sum(h - step*d, target)
               G2,F2 = smooth_value_only(h2,beta)
               if G2 < G - 1e-12 or F2 < F - 1e-9:
                   h=h2; G=G2; F=F2; accepted=True; break
           if accepted:
              alpha = min(alpha*1.04, 0.25)
           else:
              alpha *= 0.65
              stall += 1
              if alpha < 1e-4: break
       # if time and rng: perturb best? maybe continue next beta from best_h?
       if time.time() < end-5 and best_F < F:
           h=best_h.copy()
           if rng is not None:
              h=add_perturbation(h,rng,0.01)
    return best_h,best_F
```

`project_box_sum`: Use x as float copy. If target not finite? no.
```
def project_box_sum(x, target):
    x = np.asarray(x, dtype=float)
    n = x.size
    if n == 0:
       return x
    # If target close to 0 or n? handle
    if abs(target - x.sum()) < 1e-13 and np.all(x >= -1e-12) and np.all(x <= 1+1e-12):
       return np.clip(x,0,1)
    lo = -np.max(x) - 1.0
    hi = 1.0 - np.min(x) + 1.0
    # ensure sum(lo) <= target <= sum(hi): lo clip all 0; hi all 1
    # binary search
    for _ in range(45):
       mid = 0.5*(lo+hi)
       s = np.clip(x + mid, 0.0, 1.0).sum()
       if s < target:
          lo = mid
       else:
          hi = mid
    y = np.clip(x + 0.5*(lo+hi), 0.0, 1.0)
    diff = target - float(y.sum())
    if abs(diff) > 1e-12:
       if diff > 0:
          idx = np.where((y > 1e-15) & (y < 1.0 - 1e-15))[0]
          if idx.size == 0:
             idx = np.where(y < 1.0)[0]
          if idx.size:
             # distribute diff in chunks? tiny; if diff might >1e-12 maybe if projection poor
             # add to multiple if large
             total_diff = diff
             for i in idx:
                if total_diff <= 0: break
                room = 1.0 - y[i]
                add = min(room, total_diff)
                y[i] += add
                total_diff -= add
       else:
          idx = np.where((y > 1e-15) & (y < 1.0 - 1e-15))[0]
          if idx.size == 0:
             idx = np.where(y > 0.0)[0]
          total_diff = -diff
          for i in idx:
             if total_diff <= 0: break
             room = y[i]
             sub = min(room, total_diff)
             y[i] -= sub
             total_diff -= sub
    y = np.clip(y,0.0,1.0)
    return y
```
If diff large due binary search not exact? The for loop may not finish but diff small. This loop in Python could be slow if idx many, but diff small so one iteration usually. If diff not small due issue, loop up to n; okay rare.
Could optimize: target integer, y sum within 1e-9; diff small. Good.

`overlap_counts_fast`:
```
def overlap_counts_fast(h):
    h = np.asarray(h, dtype=float)
    n = h.size
    if n == 0:
       return np.array([]), np.array([])
    N = 1 << (2*n - 1).bit_length()
    H = np.fft.rfft(h, N)
    ac = np.fft.irfft(H * np.conj(H), N)
    a = ac[:n]
    # suppress tiny negative
    amin = float(a.min()) if n else 0.0
    if amin < 0.0:
       a = np.maximum(a, 0.0)
    pref = np.empty(n+1, dtype=float)
    pref[0]=0.0
    np.cumsum(h, out=pref[1:])
    U = pref[n]
    s = np.arange(n, dtype=int)
    pos = pref[n - s] - a
    neg = U - pref[s] - a
    pos = np.maximum(pos, 0.0)
    neg = np.maximum(neg, 0.0)
    return pos, neg
```
If h size 1, N = 1 << 1 =2? 2*1-1=1 bit_length1 ->2. okay.

`fast_c5`:
```
def fast_c5(h):
    n = h.size
    if n == 0: return 0.0
    pos,neg=overlap_counts_fast(h)
    m = max(float(pos.max()), float(neg.max()))
    return m*(2.0/float(n))
```

`exact_c5`:
```
def exact_c5(h):
    h = np.asarray(h, dtype=float)
    n=h.size
    if n==0: return 0.0
    dx=2.0/float(n)
    vals=np.correlate(h, 1.0 - h, mode='full')
    return float(np.max(vals)*dx)
```
Potential if vals contains negative tiny? max okay.

`smooth_stats`:
```
def smooth_stats(h,beta):
    n=int(h.size)
    dx=2.0/float(n)
    pos,neg=overlap_counts_fast(h)
    vals=np.empty(2*n-1,dtype=float)
    vals[:n]=pos*dx
    if n>1: vals[n:]=neg[1:]*dx
    m=float(vals.max())
    if beta <= 0:
       w=np.zeros(vals.size); w[int(np.argmax(vals))]=1.0; return m,w,m
    z=np.exp(beta*(vals-m))
    zsum=float(z.sum())
    if zsum <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(zsum):
       w=np.zeros(vals.size); w[int(np.argmax(vals))]=1.0; G=m
    else:
       w=z/zsum
       G=m + (1.0/beta)*float(np.log(zsum))
    return G,w,m
```
If n=1, vals length1, neg[1:] empty. okay.

`smooth_value_only`: same without w.
```
def smooth_value_only(h,beta):
    n=int(h.size); dx=2.0/n
    pos,neg=overlap_counts_fast(h)
    vals=... maybe avoid memory: compute mpos=pos.max(); if n>1 mneg=neg[1:].max(); m=max(mpos,mneg)*dx
    z=np.exp(beta*(pos*dx - m))
    if n>1: z += np.exp(beta*(neg[1:]*dx - m))
    zsum=float(np.sum(z))
    G=m + (1/beta)*log(zsum)
    return G,m
```
This creates two exp arrays; fine.

`smooth_gradient` as above.
Need ensure if `w_vals` contains nan? no. Clip negative? weights nonnegative. Use `wc = wp + wn; wc[wc<0]=0?`.
```
def smooth_gradient(h, w_vals, dx):
    n=h.size
    wp = np.array(w_vals[:n], dtype=float, copy=True)
    wn = np.zeros(n, dtype=float)
    if n > 1:
       wn[1:] = w_vals[n:]
    # remove nan
    np.nan_to_num(wp, copy=False)
    np.nan_to_num(wn, copy=False)
    mass = np.full(n, float(wp[0]), dtype=float)
    if n > 1:
       total = float(np.sum(wp[1:]))
       if total > 0.0:
          left = np.empty(n, dtype=float)
          left[0] = total
          if n > 2:
             revcum = np.cumsum(wp[1:][::-1])
             left[1:] = total - revcum[:n-1]
          mass += left
       total_neg = float(np.sum(wn[1:]))
       if total_neg > 0.0:
          cumwn = np.cumsum(wn[1:])
          mass[1:] += cumwn
    wc = wp + wn
    # cross
    if float(np.sum(wc)) > 0.0:
       L = 1 << (2*n - 1).bit_length()
       K = np.zeros(L, dtype=float)
       K[0] = 2.0 * float(wc[0])
       if n > 1:
          K[1:n] = wc[1:]
          idx = np.arange(1, n, dtype=int)
          K[L - idx] = wc[idx]
       FH = np.fft.rfft(h, L)
       FK = np.fft.rfft(K, L)
       cross = np.fft.irfft(FK * FH, L)[:n]
       mass -= cross
    mass *= dx
    return mass
```
For n=1, `L = 1 << (1).bit_length() =2`, wc0, K0; cross h. Good.
`np.nan_to_num` maybe okay.

Potential issue: `mass = np.full(n, wp[0])` then `mass += left`; for n=2, left[0]=total, left[1:] empty; mass j0 wp0+total, j1 wp0. Correct. For n=1, mass wp0.
For neg mass, `cumwn` length n-1, `mass[1:] += cumwn`. Good.

`upsample_step`:
```
def upsample_step(h, n):
    m = int(h.size)
    if n == m:
       return h.astype(float, copy=True)
    if m == 1:
       return np.full(int(n), float(h[0]))
    idx = np.floor((np.arange(n) + 0.5) * (float(m) / float(n))).astype(int)
    idx = np.clip(idx, 0, m - 1)
    return h[idx].astype(float, copy=True)
```
If h[0] not target? okay.

`symmetrize`:
```
def symmetrize(h):
    h=np.asarray(h,float)
    return 0.5*(h + h[::-1])
```

`add_perturbation`:
```
def add_perturbation(h, rng, scale=0.02):
    n=h.size
    h=np.asarray(h,float).copy()
    if rng is None: return h
    p = h + (rng.random(n, dtype=float) - 0.5) * scale
    return project_box_sum(p, 0.5*n)
```
Could type rng.random(n) works.

`make_dyadic_feasible` as above. Need `D` int power of two.
```
def make_dyadic_feasible(h, D):
    h = np.asarray(h, dtype=float)
    h = np.clip(h, 0.0, 1.0)
    n = h.size
    scaled = h * float(D)
    a = np.floor(scaled + 1e-12).astype(np.int64)
    a = np.clip(a, 0, D)
    target = (int(n) // 2) * int(D)  # n even
    # If n odd, target floor? but choose even. For safety:
    if n % 2 != 0:
       target = int(round(0.5 * n * D))
    cur = int(a.sum(dtype=np.int64))
    need = target - cur
    # compute frac for ordering: scaled - a (could be negative due floor tolerance? use fractional part)
    frac = scaled - a.astype(float)
    frac = np.where(frac < 0.0, 0.0, frac)

       if need > n: need = n
       order = np.argsort(-frac, kind='stable')
       added=0
       # To avoid Python loop O(n) okay; can vectorize partially:
       for idx in order:
          if added >= need: break
          if a[idx] < D:
             a[idx] += 1
             added += 1
       if added < need:
          rem = np.where(a < D)[0]
          take = min(need - added, rem.size)
          if take > 0:
             a[rem[:take]] += 1
    elif need < 0:
       need = -need
       if need > n: need = n
       order = np.argsort(frac, kind='stable')
       sub=0
       for idx in order:
          if sub >= need: break
          if a[idx] > 0:
             a[idx] -= 1
             sub += 1
       if sub < need:
          rem = np.where(a > 0)[0]
          take = min(need - sub, rem.size)
          if take > 0:
             a[rem[:take]] -= 1
    # final if still off (due target discrepancy), adjust arbitrary
    cur = int(a.sum(dtype=np.int64))
    need = target - cur
    if need > 0:
       rem = np.where(a < D)[0]
       take = min(need, rem.size)

def optimize_candidate(h0, budget_s, rng=None, beta_schedule=None, alpha_init=0.04):
    if budget_s <= 0:
       return project_box_sum(h0,0.5*h0.size), fast_c5(...)
    h = project_box_sum(h0, 0.5*float(h.size))
    target = 0.5*float(h.size)
    best_h = h.copy()
    best_F = fast_c5(h)
    start = time.time()
    end = start + max(0.0, budget_s)
    if beta_schedule is None: beta_schedule = (30.0, 80.0, 200.0, 500.0)
    factors = (1.0, 0.5, 0.25, 0.125, 0.0625, 0.03125, 0.015625)
    for beta in beta_schedule:
       if time.time() >= end - 3.0: break
       alpha = alpha_init
       stall = 0
       while True:
          if time.time() >= end - 3.0: break
          G, w, F = smooth_stats(h, float(beta))
          if F < best_F - 1e-12:
             best_F = F; best_h = h.copy(); stall = 0
          else:
             stall += 1
          if stall > 120:
             break
          g = smooth_gradient(h, w, 2.0/float(h.size))
          scale = float(np.max(np.abs(g)))
          if scale < 1e-12:
             break
          d = g / scale
          accepted = False
          for fac in factors:
             h2 = project_box_sum(h - (alpha * fac) * d, target)
             G2, F2 = smooth_value_only(h2, float(beta))
             if (G2 < G - 1e-11) or (F2 < F - 1e-9):
                h = h2; G = G2; F = F2; accepted = True; break
          if accepted:
             alpha = min(alpha * 1.05, 0.25)
             # do not reset stall? We reset if F improved above; if only G, stall persists? We'll not reset in accepted.
          else:
             alpha *= 0.65
             stall += 1
             if alpha < 1e-4:
                break
       if time.time() >= end - 3.0: break
       # revert to best for next beta
       h = best_h.copy()
       if rng is not None:
          h = add_perturbation(h, rng, 0.01)
          if fast_c5(h) > best_F + 1e-9:
             # keep best, but allow small perturbation? Maybe don't revert.
             h = best_h.copy()
    # final project
    h = project_box_sum(best_h, target)
    return h, fast_c5(h)
```

Random construction:
```
def random_smooth_vector(n, seed):
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
    m = max(8, n // 32)
    base = rng.standard_normal(m)
    x = np.linspace(0.5, n - 0.5, n)
    h = np.interp(x, np.linspace(0.5, m - 0.5, m), base)
    h = (h - h.min()) / (h.max() - h.min() + 1e-12)
    h = h - h.mean() + 0.5
    h = np.clip(h, 0.0, 1.0)
    return project_box_sum(h, 0.5*n)
```

```
def random_smooth_vector(n, seed):
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
    m = max(8, n // 24)
    base = rng.standard_normal(m)
    h = np.interp(np.linspace(0.5, n - 0.5, n), np.linspace(0.5, m - 0.5, m), base)
    rng2 = rng
    # scale to [0,1]
    h = (h - h.min())/(h.max()-h.min()+1e-12)
    h = h - h.mean() + 0.5
    h = np.clip(h,0,1)
    return project_box_sum(h, 0.5*n)
```

def run(seed=42,budget_s=1300, **kwargs):

      total_budget=float(budget_s)

   start=time.time()

   rng=np.random.default_rng(seed)
   parent=None

   best_h=None; best_f=float('inf'); parent_h=None; parent_f=float('inf')
   if parent is not None:

      parent_h=p.copy(); parent_f=exact_c5(p)

      parent_f? no; best_f=exact_c5(p); best_h=p

Define `initial_random_balanced` helper top-level:
```
def initial_random_balanced(n, seed):
    rng=np.random.default_rng(seed)
    h=np.zeros(n)
    ones=n//2
    if ones>0:
       idx=rng.choice(n, ones, replace=False)
       h[idx]=1.0
    return h
```

Continue run:
```
   # Build raw candidates
   raw=[]
   base = best_h if best_h is not None else None
   if base is not None and base.size > 0:
      n0=int(base.size)
      max_n=4096
      ns=[]
      for mult in (1,2,4,8,16):
         nn=n0*mult
         if 2 <= nn <= max_n and nn %2==0 and nn not in ns: ns.append(nn)
      for p in (128,256,512,1024,2048):

      ns.sort()
      for nn in ns:
         h = upsample_step(base, nn)
         h = project_box_sum(h,0.5*float(nn))
         raw.append(h)
         hs = symmetrize(h)
         hs = project_box_sum(hs,0.5*float(nn))

            hc = symmetrize(1.0 - h)

def candidate_sort_key(item):
    f = item[0]; n = item[1].size

      rem = deadline - time.time()
      if rem < 60: break
```

```python
import time
import math
import numpy as np


def project_box_sum(x, target):
    x = np.asarray(x, dtype=float)
    n = x.size
    if n == 0:
        return x

    target = float(target)
    if abs(target - float(x.sum())) < 1e-13 and np.all(x >= -1e-12) and np.all(x <= 1.0 + 1e-12):
        return np.clip(x, 0.0, 1.0)

    lo = -float(np.max(x)) - 1.0
    hi = 1.0 - float(np.min(x)) + 1.0

    for _ in range(42):
        mid = 0.5 * (lo + hi)
        s = float(np.clip(x + mid, 0.0, 1.0).sum())
        if s < target:
            lo = mid
        else:
            hi = mid

    y = np.clip(x + 0.5 * (lo + hi), 0.0, 1.0)
    diff = target - float(y.sum())

    if abs(diff) > 1e-12:
        if diff > 0:
            idx = np.where((y > 1e-15) & (y < 1.0 - 1e-15))[0]
            if idx.size == 0:
                idx = np.where(y < 1.0)[0]
            total = diff
            for i in idx:
                if total <= 0.0:
                    break
                add = min(1.0 - y[i], total)
                y[i] += add
                total -= add
        else:
            idx = np.where((y > 1e-15) & (y < 1.0 - 1e-15))[0]
            if idx.size == 0:
                idx = np.where(y > 0.0)[0]
            total = -diff
            for i in idx:
                if total <= 0.0:
                    break
                sub = min(y[i], total)
                y[i] -= sub
                total -= sub

    y = np.clip(y, 0.0, 1.0)
    return y


def exact_c5(h):
    h = np.asarray(h, dtype=float)
    n = h.size
    if n == 0:
        return 0.0
    dx = 2.0 / float(n)
    vals = np.correlate(h, 1.0 - h, mode="full")
    return float(np.max(vals) * dx)


def overlap_counts_fast(h):
    h = np.asarray(h, dtype=float)
    n = h.size
    if n == 0:
        return np.array([], dtype=float), np.array([], dtype=float)

    N = 1 << (2 * n - 1).bit_length()
    H = np.fft.rfft(h, N)
    ac = np.fft.irfft(H * np.conj(H), N)
    a = ac[:n]

    if float(np.min(a)) < 0.0:
        a = np.maximum(a, 0.0)

    pref = np.empty(n + 1, dtype=float)
    pref[0] = 0.0
    np.cumsum(h, out=pref[1:])
    U = pref[n]

    s = np.arange(n, dtype=int)
    pos = pref[n - s] - a
    neg = U - pref[s] - a

    pos = np.maximum(pos, 0.0)
    neg = np.maximum(neg, 0.0)
    return pos, neg


def fast_c5(h):
    h = np.asarray(h, dtype=float)
    n = h.size
    if n == 0:
        return 0.0
    pos, neg = overlap_counts_fast(h)
    m = max(float(np.max(pos)), float(np.max(neg)))
    return m * (2.0 / float(n))


def smooth_stats(h, beta):
    h = np.asarray(h, dtype=float)
    n = h.size
    dx = 2.0 / float(n)
    pos, neg = overlap_counts_fast(h)

    vals = np.empty(2 * n - 1, dtype=float)
    vals[:n] = pos * dx
    if n > 1:
        vals[n:] = neg[1:] * dx

    m = float(np.max(vals))

    if beta <= 0.0:
        w = np.zeros(vals.size, dtype=float)
        w[int(np.argmax(vals))] = 1.0
        return m, w, m

    z = np.exp(beta * (vals - m))
    zsum = float(np.sum(z))

    if zsum <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(zsum):
        w = np.zeros(vals.size, dtype=float)
        w[int(np.argmax(vals))] = 1.0
        G = m
    else:
        w = z / zsum
        G = m + (1.0 / beta) * float(np.log(zsum))

    return G, w, m


def smooth_value_only(h, beta):
    h = np.asarray(h, dtype=float)
    n = h.size
    dx = 2.0 / float(n)
    pos, neg = overlap_counts_fast(h)

    m = max(float(np.max(pos)), float(np.max(neg))) * dx

    z = float(np.sum(np.exp(beta * (pos * dx - m))))
    if n > 1:
        z += float(np.sum(np.exp(beta * (neg[1:] * dx - m))))

    if z <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(z):
        z = 1.0
    G = m + (1.0 / beta) * float(np.log(z))
    return G, m


def smooth_gradient(h, w_vals, dx):
    h = np.asarray(h, dtype=float)
    n = h.size

    wp = np.array(w_vals[:n], dtype=float, copy=True)
    wn = np.zeros(n, dtype=float)
    if n > 1:
        wn[1:] = w_vals[n:]

    wp = np.nan_to_num(wp)
    wn = np.nan_to_num(wn)

    mass = np.full(n, float(wp[0]), dtype=float)

    if n > 1:
        total_pos = float(np.sum(wp[1:]))
        if total_pos > 0.0:
            left = np.empty(n, dtype=float)
            left[0] = total_pos
            if n > 2:
                revcum = np.cumsum(wp[1:][::-1])
                left[1:] = total_pos - revcum[:n - 1]
            mass += left

        total_neg = float(np.sum(wn[1:]))
        if total_neg > 0.0:
            cumwn = np.cumsum(wn[1:])
            mass[1:] += cumwn

    wc = wp + wn
    if float(np.sum(wc)) > 0.0:
        L = 1 << (2 * n - 1).bit_length()
        K = np.zeros(L, dtype=float)
        K[0] = 2.0 * float(wc[0])

        if n > 1:
            K[1:n] = wc[1:]
            idx = np.arange(1, n, dtype=int)
            K[L - idx] = wc[idx]

        FH = np.fft.rfft(h, L)
        FK = np.fft.rfft(K, L)
        cross = np.fft.irfft(FK * FH, L)[:n]
        mass -= cross

    mass *= dx
    return mass


def upsample_step(h, n):
    h = np.asarray(h, dtype=float)
    m = h.size
    n = int(n)

    if n == m:
        return h.astype(float, copy=True)

    if m == 1:
        return np.full(n, float(h[0]), dtype=float)

    idx = np.floor((np.arange(n) + 0.5) * (float(m) / float(n))).astype(int)
    idx = np.clip(idx, 0, m - 1)
    return h[idx].astype(float, copy=True)


def symmetrize(h):
    h = np.asarray(h, dtype=float)
    return 0.5 * (h + h[::-1])


def top_half_binary(h):
    h = np.asarray(h, dtype=float)
    n = h.size
    out = np.zeros(n, dtype=float)
    ones = n // 2
    if ones > 0:
        order = np.argsort(-h)
        out[order[:ones]] = 1.0
    return out


def random_smooth_vector(n, seed):
    n = int(n)
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
    m = max(8, n // 24)

    base = rng.standard_normal(m)
    x = np.linspace(0.5, n - 0.5, n)
    y = np.linspace(0.5, m - 0.5, m)
    h = np.interp(x, y, base)

    if h.size:
        h = (h - float(np.min(h))) / (float(np.max(h)) - float(np.min(h)) + 1e-12)
        h = h - float(np.mean(h)) + 0.5
        h = np.clip(h, 0.0, 1.0)

    return project_box_sum(h, 0.5 * float(n))


def initial_random_balanced(n, seed):
    n = int(n)
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
    h = np.zeros(n, dtype=float)
    ones = n // 2
    if ones > 0:
        idx = rng.choice(n, ones, replace=False)
        h[idx] = 1.0
    return h


def add_perturbation(h, rng, scale=0.02):
    h = np.asarray(h, dtype=float).copy()
    n = h.size
    if rng is None or n == 0:
        return project_box_sum(h, 0.5 * float(n))

    p = h + (rng.random(n, dtype=float) - 0.5) * float(scale)
    return project_box_sum(p, 0.5 * float(n))


def optimize_candidate(h0, budget_s, rng=None, beta_schedule=None, alpha_init=0.05):
    h = np.asarray(h0, dtype=float)
    n = h.size
    if n == 0:
        return h.copy(), 0.0

    target = 0.5 * float(n)
    h = project_box_sum(h, target)

    best_h = h.copy()
    best_F = fast_c5(h)

    start = time.time()
    end = start + max(0.0, float(budget_s))

    if beta_schedule is None:
        beta_schedule = (60.0, 150.0, 400.0)

    factors = (1.0, 0.5, 0.25, 0.125, 0.0625)

    for beta in beta_schedule:
        if time.time() >= end - 3.0:
            break

        beta = float(beta)
        alpha = float(alpha_init)
        local_h = h.copy()
        local_F = fast_c5(local_h)
        prev_G = None
        stall = 0

        while True:
            if time.time() >= end - 3.0:
                break

            G, w, F = smooth_stats(local_h, beta)

            improved = False
            if F < local_F - 1e-12:
                local_F = F
                local_h = local_h.copy()
                improved = True
            if prev_G is not None and G < prev_G - 1e-10:
                improved = True

            if improved:
                stall = 0
            else:
                stall += 1

            prev_G = G
            if stall > 220:
                break

            g = smooth_gradient(local_h, w, 2.0 / float(n))
            scale = float(np.max(np.abs(g)))
            if scale < 1e-12:
                break

            d = g / scale
            accepted = False

            for fac in factors:
                h2 = project_box_sum(local_h - (alpha * fac) * d, target)
                G2, F2 = smooth_value_only(h2, beta)
                if (G2 < G - 1e-11) or (F2 < F - 1e-9):
                    local_h = h2
                    G = G2
                    F = F2
                    accepted = True
                    break

            if accepted:
                alpha = min(alpha * 1.05, 0.25)
            else:
                alpha *= 0.65
                stall += 1
                if alpha < 1e-4:
                    break

        if local_F < best_F - 1e-12:
            best_F = local_F
            best_h = local_h.copy()

        h = local_h.copy()
        if rng is not None and time.time() < end - 5.0:
            hp = add_perturbation(h, rng, 0.012)
            fp = fast_c5(hp)
            if fp < local_F + 1e-4:
                h = hp

    h = project_box_sum(best_h, target)
    return h, fast_c5(h)


def make_dyadic_feasible(h, D):
    h = np.asarray(h, dtype=float)
    h = np.clip(h, 0.0, 1.0)
    n = h.size
    D = int(D)

    scaled = h * float(D)
    a = np.floor(scaled + 1e-12).astype(np.int64)
    a = np.clip(a, 0, D)

    if n % 2 == 0:
        target = (n // 2) * D
    else:
        target = int(round(0.5 * n * D))

    cur = int(a.sum(dtype=np.int64))
    need = target - cur

    frac = scaled - a.astype(float)
    frac = np.where(frac < 0.0, 0.0, frac)

    if need > 0:
        if need > n:
            need = n
        order = np.argsort(-frac)
        added = 0
        for idx in order:
            if added >= need:
                break
            if a[idx] < D:
                a[idx] += 1
                added += 1
        if added < need:
            rem = np.where(a < D)[0]
            for i in rem:
                if added >= need:
                    break
                a[i] += 1
                added += 1

    elif need < 0:
        need = -need
        if need > n:
            need = n
        order = np.argsort(frac)
        sub = 0
        for idx in order:
            if sub >= need:
                break
            if a[idx] > 0:
                a[idx] -= 1
                sub += 1
        if sub < need:
            rem = np.where(a > 0)[0]
            for i in rem:
                if sub >= need:
                    break
                a[i] -= 1
                sub += 1

    cur = int(a.sum(dtype=np.int64))
    need = target - cur
    if need > 0:
        rem = np.where(a < D)[0]
        for i in rem:
            if need <= 0:
                break
            add = min(need, D - int(a[i]))
            a[i] += add
            need -= add
    elif need < 0:
        rem = np.where(a > 0)[0]
        for i in rem:
            if need >= 0:
                break
            sub = min(-need, int(a[i]))
            a[i] -= sub
            need += sub

    return a.astype(float) / float(D)


def choose_dyadic_denom(n):
    n = int(n)
    if n <= 4096:
        return 1 << 20
    if n <= 16384:
        return 1 << 16
    return 1 << 12


def candidate_sort_key(item):
    f = item[0]
    n = item[1].size
    return (f, n)


def run(seed=42, budget_s=1300, **kwargs):
    try:
        total_budget = float(budget_s)
    except Exception:
        total_budget = 1300.0

    start = time.time()
    deadline = start + max(1.0, total_budget) - 15.0
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)

    parent = None
    try:
        parent = initial_h_values
    except NameError:
        parent = None

    best_h = None
    best_f = float("inf")
    parent_h = None
    parent_f = float("inf")

    if parent is not None:
        p = np.asarray(parent, dtype=float)
        p = np.clip(p, 0.0, 1.0)
        p = project_box_sum(p, 0.5 * float(p.size))
        parent_h = p.copy()
        parent_f = exact_c5(p)
        best_h = p.copy()
        best_f = parent_f
    else:
        n_init = 256
        p = initial_random_balanced(n_init, seed + 101)
        best_h = p
        best_f = exact_c5(p)

    raw = []
    base = best_h
    if base is not None and base.size > 0:
        n0 = int(base.size)
        max_n = 4096

        ns = []
        for mult in (1, 2, 4, 8, 16):
            nn = n0 * mult
            if 2 <= nn <= max_n and nn % 2 == 0 and nn not in ns:
                ns.append(nn)

        for p in (128, 256, 512, 1024, 2048):
            if p >= max(2, n0) and p <= max_n and p % 2 == 0 and p not in ns:
                ns.append(p)

        ns.sort()

        for nn in ns:
            h = upsample_step(base, nn)
            h = project_box_sum(h, 0.5 * float(nn))
            raw.append(h)

            hs = symmetrize(h)
            hs = project_box_sum(hs, 0.5 * float(nn))
            raw.append(hs)

            if nn <= 256:
                hc = symmetrize(1.0 - h)
                hc = project_box_sum(hc, 0.5 * float(nn))
                raw.append(hc)

        for nn in ns[:3]:
            hb = top_half_binary(upsample_step(base, nn))
            hb = project_box_sum(hb, 0.5 * float(nn))
            raw.append(hb)

    n_rand = int(base.size) if base is not None else 256
    n_rand = max(128, min(n_rand * 2, 1024))
    if n_rand % 2:
        n_rand += 1
    hr = random_smooth_vector(n_rand, seed + 7)
    raw.append(hr)

    cands = []
    for h in raw:
        if h is None or h.size == 0:
            continue
        if not np.all(np.isfinite(h)):
            continue

        h = project_box_sum(h, 0.5 * float(h.size))

        if h.size <= 1024:
            f_est = exact_c5(h)
        else:
            f_est = fast_c5(h)

        if not np.isfinite(f_est):
            continue

        if best_f < float("inf") and f_est > best_f + 0.03:
            continue

        if f_est < best_f and h.size <= 2048:
            f_ex = exact_c5(h)
            if f_ex < best_f:
                best_f = f_ex
                best_h = h.copy()

        cands.append((f_est, h))

    cands.sort(key=candidate_sort_key)

    per_times = (90.0, 75.0, 75.0, 65.0, 60.0, 55.0, 50.0, 50.0, 45.0, 40.0, 40.0, 40.0)
    max_cand = min(len(cands), 12)

    for ci in range(max_cand):
        rem = deadline - time.time()
        if rem < 30.0:
            break

        _f_est, h0 = cands[ci]
        t = per_times[min(ci, len(per_times) - 1)]
        if t > rem - 25.0:
            t = rem - 25.0
        if t < 15.0:
            break

        h_opt, _ = optimize_candidate(h0, t, rng, (60.0, 200.0, 600.0), 0.05)
        h_opt = project_box_sum(h_opt, 0.5 * float(h_opt.size))

        f_new = exact_c5(h_opt)
        if f_new < best_f - 1e-12:
            best_f = f_new
            best_h = h_opt.copy()

    if best_h is not None and time.time() < deadline - 80.0:
        t = min(180.0, deadline - time.time() - 45.0)
        if t > 25.0:
            h_pol, _ = optimize_candidate(best_h, t, rng, (100.0, 300.0, 800.0), 0.025)
            h_pol = project_box_sum(h_pol, 0.5 * float(h_pol.size))
            fp = exact_c5(h_pol)
            if fp < best_f - 1e-12:
                best_f = fp
                best_h = h_pol.copy()

    if best_h is not None:
        n_best = int(best_h.size)
        max_n = 4096
        n2 = min(2 * n_best, max_n)

        if n2 > n_best and time.time() < deadline - 120.0:
            h_up = upsample_step(best_h, n2)
            h_up = project_box_sum(h_up, 0.5 * float(n2))
            t = min(120.0, deadline - time.time() - 60.0)
            if t > 25.0:
                h_up, _ = optimize_candidate(h_up, t, rng, (50.0, 150.0, 400.0), 0.045)
                h_up = project_box_sum(h_up, 0.5 * float(h_up.size))
                fu = exact_c5(h_up)
                if fu < best_f - 1e-12:
                    best_f = fu
                    best_h = h_up.copy()

        if best_h is not None and time.time() < deadline - 90.0:
            n_best2 = int(best_h.size)
            max_n2 = 4096
            n3 = min(2 * n_best2, max_n2)
            if n3 > n_best2:
                h_sym = symmetrize(upsample_step(best_h, n3))
                h_sym = project_box_sum(h_sym, 0.5 * float(n3))
                t = min(90.0, deadline - time.time() - 45.0)
                if t > 20.0:
                    h_sym, _ = optimize_candidate(h_sym, t, rng, (60.0, 180.0, 500.0), 0.035)
                    h_sym = project_box_sum(h_sym, 0.5 * float(h_sym.size))
                    fs = exact_c5(h_sym)
                    if fs < best_f - 1e-12:
                        best_f = fs
                        best_h = h_sym.copy()

    if parent_h is not None:
        final_h = parent_h
        final_f = parent_f
        final_n = int(parent_h.size)
    else:
        final_h = best_h
        final_f = best_f
        final_n = int(best_h.size) if best_h is not None else 0

    if best_h is not None and time.time() < deadline - 3.0:
        n_final = int(best_h.size)
        Ds = set()
        Ds.add(choose_dyadic_denom(n_final))
        Ds.add(1 << 16)
        if n_final <= 1024:
            Ds.add(1 << 20)

        rounded_h = None
        rounded_f = float("inf")

        for D in Ds:
            if time.time() >= deadline - 2.0:
                break
            hr = make_dyadic_feasible(best_h, D)
            fr = exact_c5(hr)
            if fr < rounded_f:
                rounded_f = fr
                rounded_h = hr

        if rounded_h is not None:
            if parent_h is None:
                final_h = rounded_h
                final_f = rounded_f
                final_n = int(rounded_h.size)
            else:
                if rounded_f < final_f - 1e-13:
                    final_h = rounded_h
                    final_f = rounded_f
                    final_n = int(rounded_h.size)

    if final_h is None:
        final_h = best_h
        final_f = best_f
        final_n = int(best_h.size) if best_h is not None else 0

    return final_h, float(final_f), int(final_n)
```
